Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 287 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the galactic structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 993. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
